    def test_best_match_keeps_first_place_over_its_graph_neighbours(db_session, project):
        """RAG-4, in the production shape: the best match ranks first with the graph on.
    
        On production the chunk that answers a question was semantic rank 1 and came
        15th with the graph layer on, because the layer never ranked its seeds: their
        neighbours (semantic rank 11 and worse, graph rank 2 or 3) took a graph share
        on top of their semantic one and the seed took none. Here the best match links
        to a neighbour at semantic rank 11, which that ranking put at graph rank 2
        (behind a semantic-rank-23 neighbour whose URN sorts first), and so first.
        """
        main = Document(
            id=uuid.UUID("b0000000-0000-0000-0000-000000000000"),
            project_id=project.id,
            name="Main",
            content="test",
        )
        side = Document(
            id=uuid.UUID("a0000000-0000-0000-0000-000000000000"),
            project_id=project.id,
            name="Side",
            content="test",
        )
        db_session.add_all([main, side])
        db_session.flush()
        # Semantic ranks 1-22 are chunks 0-21 of Main, rank 23 is chunk 0 of Side.
        placed = [(main, index) for index in range(22)] + [(side, 0)]
        rows = [
            DocumentChunk(document_id=document.id, chunk_index=index, content=f"text {rank}")
            for rank, (document, index) in enumerate(placed, start=1)
        ]
        db_session.add_all(rows)
        db_session.commit()
        semantic = [
            {
                "chunk_id": str(row.id),
                "content": row.content,
                "document_id": str(row.document_id),
                "project_id": str(project.id),
                "chunk_index": row.chunk_index,
                # Production cosines: 0.664 at rank 1, falling below 0.45 by rank 23.
                "score": round(0.664 - 0.01 * (rank - 1), 3),
            }
            for rank, row in enumerate(rows, start=1)
        ]
        best, neighbour, far = rows[0], rows[10], rows[22]
        for target in (neighbour, far):
            _add_edge(
                db_session,
                str(URNGenerator.for_chunk(str(best.document_id), best.chunk_index)),
                str(URNGenerator.for_chunk(str(target.document_id), target.chunk_index)),
                "related_to",
            )
    
        orchestrator = PEDRSearchOrchestrator(
            lexical_search=MagicMock(return_value=[]),
            semantic_search=MagicMock(return_value=semantic),
            graph_service=GraphLayerService(session=db_session),
            telemetry_enabled=False,
        )
        response = orchestrator.search(
            query="rag-4 best match keeps first place", top_k=23, enable_graph=True
        )
    
        ranked = {result.chunk_id: result for result in response.results}
        top = [
            (result.layer_ranks.get("semantic"), result.layer_ranks.get("graph"))
            for result in response.results[:3]
        ]
        assert response.results[0].chunk_id == str(best.id), (
            f"(semantic rank, graph rank) of the top three: {top}"
        )
        assert ranked[str(best.id)].layer_ranks == {"semantic": 1, "graph": 1}
        # The ten seeds hold the top ten in semantic order; the neighbour was reached
        # and ranks below every one of them.
        assert [result.chunk_id for result in response.results[:10]] == [
            str(row.id) for row in rows[:10]
        ]
        assert ranked[str(neighbour.id)].layer_ranks["semantic"] == 11
        assert "graph" in ranked[str(neighbour.id)].layer_ranks

CREATE TABLE graph_edges (
	id CHAR(36) NOT NULL, 
	from_urn VARCHAR(512) NOT NULL, 
	to_urn VARCHAR(512) NOT NULL, 
	edge_type VARCHAR(50) NOT NULL, 
	direction VARCHAR(20) NOT NULL, 
	weight FLOAT NOT NULL, 
	reason TEXT, 
	via VARCHAR(50), 
	evidence JSON, 
	created_at DATETIME NOT NULL, 
	updated_at DATETIME NOT NULL, 
	PRIMARY KEY (id),

CREATE TABLE missions (
	id CHAR(36) NOT NULL, 
	project_id CHAR(36), 
	mission_id VARCHAR(50) NOT NULL, 
	title VARCHAR(255) NOT NULL, 
	objective TEXT NOT NULL, 
	success_criteria JSON NOT NULL, 
	context JSON, 
	deliverables JSON, 
	research_phases JSON, 
	tags JSON, 
	mission_metadata JSON, 
	background TEXT, 
	focus TEXT, 
	"references" JSON, 
	required_entities JSON, 
	excluded_entities JSON, 
	expected_output_schema JSON, 
	coverage_thresholds JSON, 
	validation_thresholds JSON, 
	deliverable_format TEXT, 
	max_loops INTEGER, 
	min_loops INTEGER, 
	constraints JSON, 
	status VARCHAR(20) NOT NULL, 
	queued_at DATETIME, 
	started_at DATETIME, 
	completed_at DATETIME, 
	deepsearch_job_id VARCHAR(100), 
	deepsearch_lease_owner TEXT, 
	deepsearch_lease_token TEXT, 
	deepsearch_leased_at DATETIME, 
	deepsearch_heartbeat_at DATETIME, 
	deepsearch_lease_expires_at DATETIME, 
	deepsearch_attempt_count INTEGER DEFAULT '0' NOT NULL, 
	deepsearch_result_key TEXT, 
	execution_metadata JSON, 
	result_document_ids JSON, 
	result_report_id CHAR(36), 
	result_markdown TEXT, 
	result_protocol JSON, 
	error_message TEXT, 
	created_at DATETIME NOT NULL, 
	updated_at DATETIME NOT NULL, 
	created_by VARCHAR(100), 
	owner_id CHAR(36), 
	workspace_id CHAR(36), 
	PRIMARY KEY (id), 
	CONSTRAINT success_criteria_not_empty CHECK (jsonb_array_length(success_criteria) > 0), 
	CONSTRAINT title_length CHECK (length(title) >= 3 AND length(title) <= 255), 
	CONSTRAINT valid_mission_status CHECK (status IN ('draft', 'queued', 'in_progress', 'completed', 'blocked', 'cancelled', 'validation_failed')), 
	FOREIGN KEY(project_id) REFERENCES projects (id) ON DELETE CASCADE, 
	UNIQUE (mission_id), 
	FOREIGN KEY(result_report_id) REFERENCES reports (id) ON DELETE SET NULL, 
	FOREIGN KEY(owner_id) REFERENCES users (id) ON DELETE SET NULL, 
	FOREIGN KEY(workspace_id) REFERENCES workspaces (id) ON DELETE SET NULL
)

CREATE TABLE deepsearch_ledger_batches (
	id CHAR(36) NOT NULL, 
	mission_id CHAR(36) NOT NULL, 
	deepsearch_job_id VARCHAR(100) NOT NULL, 
	session_key VARCHAR(255) NOT NULL, 
	payload_hash VARCHAR(64) NOT NULL, 
	entry_count INTEGER NOT NULL, 
	created_at DATETIME DEFAULT (CURRENT_TIMESTAMP) NOT NULL, 
	updated_at DATETIME DEFAULT (CURRENT_TIMESTAMP) NOT NULL, 
	PRIMARY KEY (id), 
	CONSTRAINT ck_deepsearch_ledger_batches_nonempty_job CHECK (length(trim(deepsearch_job_id)) > 0), 
	CONSTRAINT ck_deepsearch_ledger_batches_nonempty_session CHECK (length(trim(session_key)) > 0), 
	CONSTRAINT ck_deepsearch_ledger_batches_hash_length CHECK (length(payload_hash) = 64), 
	CONSTRAINT ck_deepsearch_ledger_batches_entry_count CHECK (entry_count > 0 AND entry_count <= 1000), 
	CONSTRAINT uq_deepsearch_ledger_batches_mission_job UNIQUE (mission_id, deepsearch_job_id), 
	CONSTRAINT fk_deepsearch_ledger_batches_mission FOREIGN KEY(mission_id) REFERENCES missions (id) ON DELETE CASCADE
)

CREATE TABLE deepsearch_evidence_outbox (
	mission_id CHAR(36) NOT NULL, 
	deepsearch_job_id VARCHAR(100) NOT NULL, 
	deepsearch_result_key TEXT NOT NULL, 
	mission_attempt_count INTEGER NOT NULL, 
	terminal_status VARCHAR(32) NOT NULL, 
	schema_version SMALLINT DEFAULT 1 NOT NULL, 
	state VARCHAR(16) DEFAULT 'pending' NOT NULL, 
	delivery_attempt_count INTEGER DEFAULT 0 NOT NULL, 
	next_attempt_at DATETIME DEFAULT (CURRENT_TIMESTAMP) NOT NULL, 
	lease_token CHAR(36), 
	lease_expires_at DATETIME, 
	acked_at DATETIME, 
	last_http_status SMALLINT, 
	last_error_code VARCHAR(100), 
	created_at DATETIME DEFAULT (CURRENT_TIMESTAMP) NOT NULL, 
	updated_at DATETIME DEFAULT (CURRENT_TIMESTAMP) NOT NULL, 
	CONSTRAINT pk_deepsearch_evidence_outbox PRIMARY KEY (mission_id, deepsearch_job_id), 
	CONSTRAINT ck_deepsearch_evidence_outbox_nonempty_job CHECK (length(trim(deepsearch_job_id)) > 0), 
	CONSTRAINT ck_deepsearch_evidence_outbox_nonempty_result_key CHECK (length(trim(deepsearch_result_key)) > 0), 
	CONSTRAINT ck_deepsearch_evidence_outbox_positive_attempt CHECK (mission_attempt_count > 0), 
	CONSTRAINT ck_deepsearch_evidence_outbox_terminal_status CHECK (terminal_status IN ('completed', 'validation_failed')), 
	CONSTRAINT ck_deepsearch_evidence_outbox_schema_version CHECK (schema_version = 1), 
	CONSTRAINT ck_deepsearch_evidence_outbox_state CHECK (state IN ('pending', 'leased', 'acked', 'dead_letter')), 
	CONSTRAINT ck_deepsearch_evidence_outbox_delivery_attempts CHECK (delivery_attempt_count >= 0), 
	CONSTRAINT ck_deepsearch_evidence_outbox_http_status CHECK (last_http_status IS NULL OR (last_http_status >= 100 AND last_http_status <= 599)), 
	CONSTRAINT ck_deepsearch_evidence_outbox_state_coherence CHECK ((state = 'leased' AND lease_token IS NOT NULL AND lease_expires_at IS NOT NULL AND next_attempt_at = lease_expires_at AND acked_at IS NULL) OR (state = 'acked' AND lease_token IS NULL AND lease_expires_at IS NULL AND acked_at IS NOT NULL) OR (state IN ('pending', 'dead_letter') AND lease_token IS NULL AND lease_expires_at IS NULL AND acked_at IS NULL)), 
	CONSTRAINT fk_deepsearch_evidence_outbox_mission FOREIGN KEY(mission_id) REFERENCES missions (id) ON DELETE CASCADE
)

CREATE TABLE ledger_entries (
	id CHAR(36) NOT NULL, 
	project_id CHAR(36) NOT NULL, 
	mission_id CHAR(36), 
	deepsearch_batch_id CHAR(36), 
	session_key VARCHAR(255) NOT NULL, 
	origin VARCHAR(32) DEFAULT 'mcp-agent' NOT NULL, 
	claim TEXT NOT NULL, 
	summary TEXT, 
	source_url TEXT NOT NULL, 
	source_id CHAR(36) NOT NULL, 
	snippet TEXT, 
	"query" TEXT, 
	disposition VARCHAR(32) NOT NULL, 
	tags JSON DEFAULT '[]' NOT NULL, 
	owner_id CHAR(36), 
	workspace_id CHAR(36), 
	created_at DATETIME DEFAULT (CURRENT_TIMESTAMP) NOT NULL, 
	updated_at DATETIME DEFAULT (CURRENT_TIMESTAMP) NOT NULL, 
	PRIMARY KEY (id), 
	CONSTRAINT fk_ledger_entries_source_project FOREIGN KEY(source_id, project_id) REFERENCES ledger_sources (id, project_id) ON DELETE NO ACTION DEFERRABLE INITIALLY DEFERRED, 
	CONSTRAINT ck_ledger_entries_origin CHECK (origin IN ('mcp-agent', 'deepsearch-worker')), 
	CONSTRAINT ck_ledger_entries_disposition CHECK (disposition IN ('supporting', 'contradicting', 'rejected', 'background')), 
	CONSTRAINT ck_ledger_entries_nonempty_session CHECK (length(trim(session_key)) > 0), 
	CONSTRAINT ck_ledger_entries_nonempty_claim CHECK (length(trim(claim)) > 0), 
	CONSTRAINT ck_ledger_entries_nonempty_source_url CHECK (length(trim(source_url)) > 0), 
	FOREIGN KEY(project_id) REFERENCES projects (id) ON DELETE CASCADE, 
	FOREIGN KEY(mission_id) REFERENCES missions (id) ON DELETE SET NULL, 
	CONSTRAINT fk_ledger_entries_deepsearch_batch FOREIGN KEY(deepsearch_batch_id) REFERENCES deepsearch_ledger_batches (id) ON DELETE SET NULL, 
	FOREIGN KEY(owner_id) REFERENCES users (id) ON DELETE SET NULL, 
	FOREIGN KEY(workspace_id) REFERENCES workspaces (id) ON DELETE SET NULL
)

CREATE TABLE graph_edges (
	id CHAR(36) NOT NULL, 
	from_urn VARCHAR(512) NOT NULL, 
	to_urn VARCHAR(512) NOT NULL, 
	edge_type VARCHAR(50) NOT NULL, 
	direction VARCHAR(20) NOT NULL, 
	weight FLOAT NOT NULL, 
	reason TEXT, 
	via VARCHAR(50), 
	evidence JSON, 
	created_at DATETIME NOT NULL, 
	updated_at DATETIME NOT NULL, 
	PRIMARY KEY (id), 
	CONSTRAINT uq_graph_edges_from_to_type_direction UNIQUE (from_urn, to_urn, edge_type, direction)
)

CREATE TABLE missions (
	id CHAR(36) NOT NULL, 
	project_id CHAR(36), 
	mission_id VARCHAR(50) NOT NULL, 
	title VARCHAR(255) NOT NULL, 
	objective TEXT NOT NULL, 
	success_criteria JSON NOT NULL, 
	context JSON, 
	deliverables JSON, 
	research_phases JSON, 
	tags JSON, 
	mission_metadata JSON, 
	background TEXT, 
	focus TEXT, 
	"references" JSON, 
	required_entities JSON, 
	excluded_entities JSON, 
	expected_output_schema JSON, 
	coverage_thresholds JSON, 
	validation_thresholds JSON, 
	deliverable_format TEXT, 
	max_loops INTEGER, 
	min_loops INTEGER, 
	constraints JSON, 
	status VARCHAR(20) NOT NULL, 
	queued_at DATETIME, 
	started_at DATETIME, 
	completed_at DATETIME, 
	deepsearch_job_id VARCHAR(100), 
	deepsearch_lease_owner TEXT, 
	deepsearch_lease_token TEXT, 
	deepsearch_leased_at DATETIME, 
	deepsearch_heartbeat_at DATETIME, 
	deepsearch_lease_expires_at DATETIME, 
	deepsearch_attempt_count INTEGER DEFAULT '0' NOT NULL, 
	deepsearch_result_key TEXT, 
	execution_metadata JSON, 
	result_document_ids JSON, 
	result_report_id CHAR(36), 
	result_markdown TEXT, 
	result_protocol JSON, 
	error_message TEXT, 
	created_at DATETIME NOT NULL, 
	updated_at DATETIME NOT NULL, 
	created_by VARCHAR(100), 
	owner_id CHAR(36), 
	workspace_id CHAR(36), 
	PRIMARY KEY (id), 
	CONSTRAINT success_criteria_not_empty CHECK (jsonb_array_length(success_criteria) > 0), 
	CONSTRAINT title_length CHECK (length(title) >= 3 AND length(title) <= 255), 
	CONSTRAINT valid_mission_status CHECK (status IN ('draft', 'queued', 'in_progress', 'completed', 'blocked', 'cancelled', 'validation_failed')), 
	FOREIGN KEY(project_id) REFERENCES projects (id) ON DELETE CASCADE, 
	UNIQUE (mission_id), 
	FOREIGN KEY(result_report_id) REFERENCES reports (id) ON DELETE SET NULL, 
	FOREIGN KEY(owner_id) REFERENCES users (id) ON DELETE SET NULL, 
	FOREIGN KEY(workspace_id) REFERENCES workspaces (id) ON DELETE SET NULL
)

CREATE TABLE deepsearch_evidence_outbox (
	mission_id CHAR(36) NOT NULL, 
	deepsearch_job_id VARCHAR(100) NOT NULL, 
	deepsearch_result_key TEXT NOT NULL, 
	mission_attempt_count INTEGER NOT NULL, 
	terminal_status VARCHAR(32) NOT NULL, 
	schema_version SMALLINT DEFAULT 1 NOT NULL, 
	state VARCHAR(16) DEFAULT 'pending' NOT NULL, 
	delivery_attempt_count INTEGER DEFAULT 0 NOT NULL, 
	next_attempt_at DATETIME DEFAULT (CURRENT_TIMESTAMP) NOT NULL, 
	lease_token CHAR(36), 
	lease_expires_at DATETIME, 
	acked_at DATETIME, 
	last_http_status SMALLINT, 
	last_error_code VARCHAR(100), 
	created_at DATETIME DEFAULT (CURRENT_TIMESTAMP) NOT NULL, 
	updated_at DATETIME DEFAULT (CURRENT_TIMESTAMP) NOT NULL, 
	CONSTRAINT pk_deepsearch_evidence_outbox PRIMARY KEY (mission_id, deepsearch_job_id), 
	CONSTRAINT ck_deepsearch_evidence_outbox_nonempty_job CHECK (length(trim(deepsearch_job_id)) > 0), 
	CONSTRAINT ck_deepsearch_evidence_outbox_nonempty_result_key CHECK (length(trim(deepsearch_result_key)) > 0), 
	CONSTRAINT ck_deepsearch_evidence_outbox_positive_attempt CHECK (mission_attempt_count > 0), 
	CONSTRAINT ck_deepsearch_evidence_outbox_terminal_status CHECK (terminal_status IN ('completed', 'validation_failed')), 
	CONSTRAINT ck_deepsearch_evidence_outbox_schema_version CHECK (schema_version = 1), 
	CONSTRAINT ck_deepsearch_evidence_outbox_state CHECK (state IN ('pending', 'leased', 'acked', 'dead_letter')), 
	CONSTRAINT ck_deepsearch_evidence_outbox_delivery_attempts CHECK (delivery_attempt_count >= 0), 
	CONSTRAINT ck_deepsearch_evidence_outbox_http_status CHECK (last_http_status IS NULL OR (last_http_status >= 100 AND last_http_status <= 599)), 
	CONSTRAINT ck_deepsearch_evidence_outbox_state_coherence CHECK ((state = 'leased' AND lease_token IS NOT NULL AND lease_expires_at IS NOT NULL AND next_attempt_at = lease_expires_at AND acked_at IS NULL) OR (state = 'acked' AND lease_token IS NULL AND lease_expires_at IS NULL AND acked_at IS NOT NULL) OR (state IN ('pending', 'dead_letter') AND lease_token IS NULL AND lease_expires_at IS NULL AND acked_at IS NULL)), 
	CONSTRAINT fk_deepsearch_evidence_outbox_mission FOREIGN KEY(mission_id) REFERENCES missions (id) ON DELETE CASCADE
)

CREATE TABLE ledger_entries (
	id CHAR(36) NOT NULL, 
	project_id CHAR(36) NOT NULL, 
	mission_id CHAR(36), 
	deepsearch_batch_id CHAR(36), 
	session_key VARCHAR(255) NOT NULL, 
	origin VARCHAR(32) DEFAULT 'mcp-agent' NOT NULL, 
	claim TEXT NOT NULL, 
	summary TEXT, 
	source_url TEXT NOT NULL, 
	source_id CHAR(36) NOT NULL, 
	snippet TEXT, 
	"query" TEXT, 
	disposition VARCHAR(32) NOT NULL, 
	tags JSON DEFAULT '[]' NOT NULL, 
	owner_id CHAR(36), 
	workspace_id CHAR(36), 
	created_at DATETIME DEFAULT (CURRENT_TIMESTAMP) NOT NULL, 
	updated_at DATETIME DEFAULT (CURRENT_TIMESTAMP) NOT NULL, 
	PRIMARY KEY (id), 
	CONSTRAINT fk_ledger_entries_source_project FOREIGN KEY(source_id, project_id) REFERENCES ledger_sources (id, project_id) ON DELETE NO ACTION DEFERRABLE INITIALLY DEFERRED, 
	CONSTRAINT ck_ledger_entries_origin CHECK (origin IN ('mcp-agent', 'deepsearch-worker')), 
	CONSTRAINT ck_ledger_entries_disposition CHECK (disposition IN ('supporting', 'contradicting', 'rejected', 'background')), 
	CONSTRAINT ck_ledger_entries_nonempty_session CHECK (length(trim(session_key)) > 0), 
	CONSTRAINT ck_ledger_entries_nonempty_claim CHECK (length(trim(claim)) > 0), 
	CONSTRAINT ck_ledger_entries_nonempty_source_url CHECK (length(trim(source_url)) > 0), 
	FOREIGN KEY(project_id) REFERENCES projects (id) ON DELETE CASCADE, 
	FOREIGN KEY(mission_id) REFERENCES missions (id) ON DELETE SET NULL, 
	CONSTRAINT fk_ledger_entries_deepsearch_batch FOREIGN KEY(deepsearch_batch_id) REFERENCES deepsearch_ledger_batches (id) ON DELETE SET NULL, 
	FOREIGN KEY(owner_id) REFERENCES users (id) ON DELETE SET NULL, 
	FOREIGN KEY(workspace_id) REFERENCES workspaces (id) ON DELETE SET NULL
)

CREATE TABLE graph_edges (
	id CHAR(36) NOT NULL, 
	from_urn VARCHAR(512) NOT NULL, 
	to_urn VARCHAR(512) NOT NULL, 
	edge_type VARCHAR(50) NOT NULL, 
	direction VARCHAR(20) NOT NULL, 
	weight FLOAT NOT NULL, 
	reason TEXT, 
	via VARCHAR(50), 
	evidence JSON, 
	created_at DATETIME NOT NULL, 
	updated_at DATETIME NOT NULL, 
	PRIMARY KEY (id), 
	CONSTRAINT uq_graph_edges_from_to_type_direction UNIQUE (from_urn, to_urn, edge_type, direction)
)

CREATE TABLE missions (
	id CHAR(36) NOT NULL, 
	project_id CHAR(36), 
	mission_id VARCHAR(50) NOT NULL, 
	title VARCHAR(255) NOT NULL, 
	objective TEXT NOT NULL, 
	success_criteria JSON NOT NULL, 
	context JSON, 
	deliverables JSON, 
	research_phases JSON, 
	tags JSON, 
	mission_metadata JSON, 
	background TEXT, 
	focus TEXT, 
	"references" JSON, 
	required_entities JSON, 
	excluded_entities JSON, 
	expected_output_schema JSON, 
	coverage_thresholds JSON, 
	validation_thresholds JSON, 
	deliverable_format TEXT, 
	max_loops INTEGER, 
	min_loops INTEGER, 
	constraints JSON, 
	status VARCHAR(20) NOT NULL, 
	queued_at DATETIME, 
	started_at DATETIME, 
	completed_at DATETIME, 
	deepsearch_job_id VARCHAR(100), 
	deepsearch_lease_owner TEXT, 
	deepsearch_lease_token TEXT, 
	deepsearch_leased_at DATETIME, 
	deepsearch_heartbeat_at DATETIME, 
	deepsearch_lease_expires_at DATETIME, 
	deepsearch_attempt_count INTEGER DEFAULT '0' NOT NULL, 
	deepsearch_result_key TEXT, 
	execution_metadata JSON, 
	result_document_ids JSON, 
	result_report_id CHAR(36), 
	result_markdown TEXT, 
	result_protocol JSON, 
	error_message TEXT, 
	created_at DATETIME NOT NULL, 
	updated_at DATETIME NOT NULL, 
	created_by VARCHAR(100), 
	owner_id CHAR(36), 
	workspace_id CHAR(36), 
	PRIMARY KEY (id), 
	CONSTRAINT success_criteria_not_empty CHECK (jsonb_array_length(success_criteria) > 0), 
	CONSTRAINT title_length CHECK (length(title) >= 3 AND length(title) <= 255), 
	CONSTRAINT valid_mission_status CHECK (status IN ('draft', 'queued', 'in_progress', 'completed', 'blocked', 'cancelled', 'validation_failed')),

	FOREIGN KEY(workspace_id) REFERENCES workspaces (id) ON DELETE SET NULL

CREATE TABLE deepsearch_ledger_batches (
	id CHAR(36) NOT NULL, 
	mission_id CHAR(36) NOT NULL, 
	deepsearch_job_id VARCHAR(100) NOT NULL, 
	session_key VARCHAR(255) NOT NULL, 
	payload_hash VARCHAR(64) NOT NULL, 
	entry_count INTEGER NOT NULL, 
	created_at DATETIME DEFAULT (CURRENT_TIMESTAMP) NOT NULL, 
	updated_at DATETIME DEFAULT (CURRENT_TIMESTAMP) NOT NULL, 
	PRIMARY KEY (id), 
	CONSTRAINT ck_deepsearch_ledger_batches_nonempty_job CHECK (length(trim(deepsearch_job_id)) > 0), 
	CONSTRAINT ck_deepsearch_ledger_batches_nonempty_session CHECK (length(trim(session_key)) > 0), 
	CONSTRAINT ck_deepsearch_ledger_batches_hash_length CHECK (length(payload_hash) = 64), 
	CONSTRAINT ck_deepsearch_ledger_batches_entry_count CHECK (entry_count > 0 AND entry_count <= 1000), 
	CONSTRAINT uq_deepsearch_ledger_batches_mission_job UNIQUE (mission_id, deepsearch_job_id), 
	CONSTRAINT fk_deepsearch_ledger_batches_mission FOREIGN KEY(mission_id) REFERENCES missions (id) ON DELETE CASCADE
)

CREATE TABLE deepsearch_evidence_outbox (
	mission_id CHAR(36) NOT NULL, 
	deepsearch_job_id VARCHAR(100) NOT NULL, 
	deepsearch_result_key TEXT NOT NULL, 
	mission_attempt_count INTEGER NOT NULL, 
	terminal_status VARCHAR(32) NOT NULL, 
	schema_version SMALLINT DEFAULT 1 NOT NULL, 
	state VARCHAR(16) DEFAULT 'pending' NOT NULL, 
	delivery_attempt_count INTEGER DEFAULT 0 NOT NULL, 
	next_attempt_at DATETIME DEFAULT (CURRENT_TIMESTAMP) NOT NULL, 
	lease_token CHAR(36), 
	lease_expires_at DATETIME, 
	acked_at DATETIME, 
	last_http_status SMALLINT, 
	last_error_code VARCHAR(100), 
	created_at DATETIME DEFAULT (CURRENT_TIMESTAMP) NOT NULL, 
	updated_at DATETIME DEFAULT (CURRENT_TIMESTAMP) NOT NULL, 
	CONSTRAINT pk_deepsearch_evidence_outbox PRIMARY KEY (mission_id, deepsearch_job_id), 
	CONSTRAINT ck_deepsearch_evidence_outbox_nonempty_job CHECK (length(trim(deepsearch_job_id)) > 0), 
	CONSTRAINT ck_deepsearch_evidence_outbox_nonempty_result_key CHECK (length(trim(deepsearch_result_key)) > 0), 
	CONSTRAINT ck_deepsearch_evidence_outbox_positive_attempt CHECK (mission_attempt_count > 0), 
	CONSTRAINT ck_deepsearch_evidence_outbox_terminal_status CHECK (terminal_status IN ('completed', 'validation_failed')), 
	CONSTRAINT ck_deepsearch_evidence_outbox_schema_version CHECK (schema_version = 1), 
	CONSTRAINT ck_deepsearch_evidence_outbox_state CHECK (state IN ('pending', 'leased', 'acked', 'dead_letter')), 
	CONSTRAINT ck_deepsearch_evidence_outbox_delivery_attempts CHECK (delivery_attempt_count >= 0), 
	CONSTRAINT ck_deepsearch_evidence_outbox_http_status CHECK (last_http_status IS NULL OR (last_http_status >= 100 AND last_http_status <= 599)), 
	CONSTRAINT ck_deepsearch_evidence_outbox_state_coherence CHECK ((state = 'leased' AND lease_token IS NOT NULL AND lease_expires_at IS NOT NULL AND next_attempt_at = lease_expires_at AND acked_at IS NULL) OR (state = 'acked' AND lease_token IS NULL AND lease_expires_at IS NULL AND acked_at IS NOT NULL) OR (state IN ('pending', 'dead_letter') AND lease_token IS NULL AND lease_expires_at IS NULL AND acked_at IS NULL)), 
	CONSTRAINT fk_deepsearch_evidence_outbox_mission FOREIGN KEY(mission_id) REFERENCES missions (id) ON DELETE CASCADE
)

CREATE TABLE ledger_entries (
	id CHAR(36) NOT NULL, 
	project_id CHAR(36) NOT NULL, 
	mission_id CHAR(36), 
	deepsearch_batch_id CHAR(36), 
	session_key VARCHAR(255) NOT NULL, 
	origin VARCHAR(32) DEFAULT 'mcp-agent' NOT NULL, 
	claim TEXT NOT NULL, 
	summary TEXT, 
	source_url TEXT NOT NULL, 
	source_id CHAR(36) NOT NULL, 
	snippet TEXT, 
	"query" TEXT, 
	disposition VARCHAR(32) NOT NULL, 
	tags JSON DEFAULT '[]' NOT NULL, 
	owner_id CHAR(36), 
	workspace_id CHAR(36), 
	created_at DATETIME DEFAULT (CURRENT_TIMESTAMP) NOT NULL, 
	updated_at DATETIME DEFAULT (CURRENT_TIMESTAMP) NOT NULL, 
	PRIMARY KEY (id), 
	CONSTRAINT fk_ledger_entries_source_project FOREIGN KEY(source_id, project_id) REFERENCES ledger_sources (id, project_id) ON DELETE NO ACTION DEFERRABLE INITIALLY DEFERRED, 
	CONSTRAINT ck_ledger_entries_origin CHECK (origin IN ('mcp-agent', 'deepsearch-worker')), 
	CONSTRAINT ck_ledger_entries_disposition CHECK (disposition IN ('supporting', 'contradicting', 'rejected', 'background')), 
	CONSTRAINT ck_ledger_entries_nonempty_session CHECK (length(trim(session_key)) > 0), 
	CONSTRAINT ck_ledger_entries_nonempty_claim CHECK (length(trim(claim)) > 0), 
	CONSTRAINT ck_ledger_entries_nonempty_source_url CHECK (length(trim(source_url)) > 0), 
	FOREIGN KEY(project_id) REFERENCES projects (id) ON DELETE CASCADE, 
	FOREIGN KEY(mission_id) REFERENCES missions (id) ON DELETE SET NULL, 
	CONSTRAINT fk_ledger_entries_deepsearch_batch FOREIGN KEY(deepsearch_batch_id) REFERENCES deepsearch_ledger_batches (id) ON DELETE SET NULL, 
	FOREIGN KEY(owner_id) REFERENCES users (id) ON DELETE SET NULL, 
	FOREIGN KEY(workspace_id) REFERENCES workspaces (id) ON DELETE SET NULL
)

CREATE TABLE graph_edges (
	id CHAR(36) NOT NULL, 
	from_urn VARCHAR(512) NOT NULL, 
	to_urn VARCHAR(512) NOT NULL, 
	edge_type VARCHAR(50) NOT NULL, 
	direction VARCHAR(20) NOT NULL, 
	weight FLOAT NOT NULL, 
	reason TEXT, 
	via VARCHAR(50), 
	evidence JSON, 
	created_at DATETIME NOT NULL, 
	updated_at DATETIME NOT NULL, 
	PRIMARY KEY (id), 
	CONSTRAINT uq_graph_edges_from_to_type_direction UNIQUE (from_urn, to_urn, edge_type, direction)
)

CREATE TABLE missions (
	id CHAR(36) NOT NULL, 
	project_id CHAR(36), 
	mission_id VARCHAR(50) NOT NULL, 
	title VARCHAR(255) NOT NULL, 
	objective TEXT NOT NULL, 
	success_criteria JSON NOT NULL, 
	context JSON, 
	deliverables JSON, 
	research_phases JSON, 
	tags JSON, 
	mission_metadata JSON, 
	background TEXT, 
	focus TEXT, 
	"references" JSON, 
	required_entities JSON, 
	excluded_entities JSON, 
	expected_output_schema JSON, 
	coverage_thresholds JSON, 
	validation_thresholds JSON, 
	deliverable_format TEXT, 
	max_loops INTEGER, 
	min_loops INTEGER, 
	constraints JSON, 
	status VARCHAR(20) NOT NULL, 
	queued_at DATETIME, 
	started_at DATETIME, 
	completed_at DATETIME, 
	deepsearch_job_id VARCHAR(100), 
	deepsearch_lease_owner TEXT, 
	deepsearch_lease_token TEXT, 
	deepsearch_leased_at DATETIME, 
	deepsearch_heartbeat_at DATETIME, 
	deepsearch_lease_expires_at DATETIME, 
	deepsearch_attempt_count INTEGER DEFAULT '0' NOT NULL, 
	deepsearch_result_key TEXT, 
	execution_metadata JSON, 
	result_document_ids JSON, 
	result_report_id CHAR(36), 
	result_markdown TEXT, 
	result_protocol JSON, 
	error_message TEXT, 
	created_at DATETIME NOT NULL, 
	updated_at DATETIME NOT NULL, 
	created_by VARCHAR(100), 
	owner_id CHAR(36), 
	workspace_id CHAR(36), 
	PRIMARY KEY (id), 
	CONSTRAINT success_criteria_not_empty CHECK (jsonb_array_length(success_criteria) > 0), 
	CONSTRAINT title_length CHECK (length(title) >= 3 AND length(title) <= 255), 
	CONSTRAINT valid_mission_status CHECK (status IN ('draft', 'queued', 'in_progress', 'completed', 'blocked', 'cancelled', 'validation_failed')), 
	FOREIGN KEY(project_id) REFERENCES projects (id) ON DELETE CASCADE, 
	UNIQUE (mission_id), 
	FOREIGN KEY(result_report_id) REFERENCES reports (id) ON DELETE SET NULL, 
	FOREIGN KEY(owner_id) REFERENCES users (id) ON DELETE SET NULL, 
	FOREIGN KEY(workspace_id) REFERENCES workspaces (id) ON DELETE SET NULL
)

CREATE TABLE deepsearch_evidence_outbox (
	mission_id CHAR(36) NOT NULL, 
	deepsearch_job_id VARCHAR(100) NOT NULL, 
	deepsearch_result_key TEXT NOT NULL, 
	mission_attempt_count INTEGER NOT NULL, 
	terminal_status VARCHAR(32) NOT NULL, 
	schema_version SMALLINT DEFAULT 1 NOT NULL, 
	state VARCHAR(16) DEFAULT 'pending' NOT NULL, 
	delivery_attempt_count INTEGER DEFAULT 0 NOT NULL, 
	next_attempt_at DATETIME DEFAULT (CURRENT_TIMESTAMP) NOT NULL, 
	lease_token CHAR(36), 
	lease_expires_at DATETIME, 
	acked_at DATETIME, 
	last_http_status SMALLINT, 
	last_error_code VARCHAR(100), 
	created_at DATETIME DEFAULT (CURRENT_TIMESTAMP) NOT NULL, 
	updated_at DATETIME DEFAULT (CURRENT_TIMESTAMP) NOT NULL, 
	CONSTRAINT pk_deepsearch_evidence_outbox PRIMARY KEY (mission_id, deepsearch_job_id), 
	CONSTRAINT ck_deepsearch_evidence_outbox_nonempty_job CHECK (length(trim(deepsearch_job_id)) > 0), 
	CONSTRAINT ck_deepsearch_evidence_outbox_nonempty_result_key CHECK (length(trim(deepsearch_result_key)) > 0), 
	CONSTRAINT ck_deepsearch_evidence_outbox_positive_attempt CHECK (mission_attempt_count > 0), 
	CONSTRAINT ck_deepsearch_evidence_outbox_terminal_status CHECK (terminal_status IN ('completed', 'validation_failed')), 
	CONSTRAINT ck_deepsearch_evidence_outbox_schema_version CHECK (schema_version = 1), 
	CONSTRAINT ck_deepsearch_evidence_outbox_state CHECK (state IN ('pending', 'leased', 'acked', 'dead_letter')), 
	CONSTRAINT ck_deepsearch_evidence_outbox_delivery_attempts CHECK (delivery_attempt_count >= 0), 
	CONSTRAINT ck_deepsearch_evidence_outbox_http_status CHECK (last_http_status IS NULL OR (last_http_status >= 100 AND last_http_status <= 599)), 
	CONSTRAINT ck_deepsearch_evidence_outbox_state_coherence CHECK ((state = 'leased' AND lease_token IS NOT NULL AND lease_expires_at IS NOT NULL AND next_attempt_at = lease_expires_at AND acked_at IS NULL) OR (state = 'acked' AND lease_token IS NULL AND lease_expires_at IS NULL AND acked_at IS NOT NULL) OR (state IN ('pending', 'dead_letter') AND lease_token IS NULL AND lease_expires_at IS NULL AND acked_at IS NULL)), 
	CONSTRAINT fk_deepsearch_evidence_outbox_mission FOREIGN KEY(mission_id) REFERENCES missions (id) ON DELETE CASCADE
)

	mission_id CHAR(36),

	attribution VARCHAR(32) NOT NULL, 
	provider VARCHAR(64), 
	model VARCHAR(128), 
	input_tokens INTEGER, 
	output_tokens INTEGER, 
	total_tokens INTEGER, 
	requests INTEGER, 
	steps INTEGER, 
	tool_calls INTEGER, 
	duration_seconds FLOAT, 
	usage_complete BOOLEAN, 
	cost_usd NUMERIC(12, 6), 
	details JSON, 
	started_at DATETIME,

CREATE TABLE ledger_entries (
	id CHAR(36) NOT NULL, 
	project_id CHAR(36) NOT NULL, 
	mission_id CHAR(36), 
	deepsearch_batch_id CHAR(36), 
	session_key VARCHAR(255) NOT NULL, 
	origin VARCHAR(32) DEFAULT 'mcp-agent' NOT NULL, 
	claim TEXT NOT NULL, 
	summary TEXT, 
	source_url TEXT NOT NULL, 
	source_id CHAR(36) NOT NULL, 
	snippet TEXT, 
	"query" TEXT, 
	disposition VARCHAR(32) NOT NULL, 
	tags JSON DEFAULT '[]' NOT NULL, 
	owner_id CHAR(36), 
	workspace_id CHAR(36), 
	created_at DATETIME DEFAULT (CURRENT_TIMESTAMP) NOT NULL, 
	updated_at DATETIME DEFAULT (CURRENT_TIMESTAMP) NOT NULL, 
	PRIMARY KEY (id), 
	CONSTRAINT fk_ledger_entries_source_project FOREIGN KEY(source_id, project_id) REFERENCES ledger_sources (id, project_id) ON DELETE NO ACTION DEFERRABLE INITIALLY DEFERRED, 
	CONSTRAINT ck_ledger_entries_origin CHECK (origin IN ('mcp-agent', 'deepsearch-worker')), 
	CONSTRAINT ck_ledger_entries_disposition CHECK (disposition IN ('supporting', 'contradicting', 'rejected', 'background')), 
	CONSTRAINT ck_ledger_entries_nonempty_session CHECK (length(trim(session_key)) > 0), 
	CONSTRAINT ck_ledger_entries_nonempty_claim CHECK (length(trim(claim)) > 0), 
	CONSTRAINT ck_ledger_entries_nonempty_source_url CHECK (length(trim(source_url)) > 0), 
	FOREIGN KEY(project_id) REFERENCES projects (id) ON DELETE CASCADE, 
	FOREIGN KEY(mission_id) REFERENCES missions (id) ON DELETE SET NULL, 
	CONSTRAINT fk_ledger_entries_deepsearch_batch FOREIGN KEY(deepsearch_batch_id) REFERENCES deepsearch_ledger_batches (id) ON DELETE SET NULL, 
	FOREIGN KEY(owner_id) REFERENCES users (id) ON DELETE SET NULL, 
	FOREIGN KEY(workspace_id) REFERENCES workspaces (id) ON DELETE SET NULL
)

CREATE TABLE graph_edges (
	id CHAR(36) NOT NULL, 
	from_urn VARCHAR(512) NOT NULL, 
	to_urn VARCHAR(512) NOT NULL, 
	edge_type VARCHAR(50) NOT NULL, 
	direction VARCHAR(20) NOT NULL, 
	weight FLOAT NOT NULL, 
	reason TEXT, 
	via VARCHAR(50), 
	evidence JSON, 
	created_at DATETIME NOT NULL, 
	updated_at DATETIME NOT NULL, 
	PRIMARY KEY (id), 
	CONSTRAINT uq_graph_edges_from_to_type_direction UNIQUE (from_urn, to_urn, edge_type, direction)
)

	id CHAR(36) NOT NULL, 
	email VARCHAR(255) NOT NULL, 
	display_name VARCHAR(255) NOT NULL, 
	password_hash VARCHAR(255) NOT NULL,

CREATE TABLE missions (
	id CHAR(36) NOT NULL, 
	project_id CHAR(36), 
	mission_id VARCHAR(50) NOT NULL, 
	title VARCHAR(255) NOT NULL, 
	objective TEXT NOT NULL, 
	success_criteria JSON NOT NULL, 
	context JSON, 
	deliverables JSON, 
	research_phases JSON, 
	tags JSON, 
	mission_metadata JSON, 
	background TEXT, 
	focus TEXT, 
	"references" JSON, 
	required_entities JSON, 
	excluded_entities JSON, 
	expected_output_schema JSON, 
	coverage_thresholds JSON, 
	validation_thresholds JSON, 
	deliverable_format TEXT, 
	max_loops INTEGER, 
	min_loops INTEGER, 
	constraints JSON, 
	status VARCHAR(20) NOT NULL, 
	queued_at DATETIME, 
	started_at DATETIME, 
	completed_at DATETIME, 
	deepsearch_job_id VARCHAR(100), 
	deepsearch_lease_owner TEXT, 
	deepsearch_lease_token TEXT, 
	deepsearch_leased_at DATETIME, 
	deepsearch_heartbeat_at DATETIME, 
	deepsearch_lease_expires_at DATETIME, 
	deepsearch_attempt_count INTEGER DEFAULT '0' NOT NULL, 
	deepsearch_result_key TEXT, 
	execution_metadata JSON, 
	result_document_ids JSON, 
	result_report_id CHAR(36), 
	result_markdown TEXT, 
	result_protocol JSON, 
	error_message TEXT, 
	created_at DATETIME NOT NULL, 
	updated_at DATETIME NOT NULL, 
	created_by VARCHAR(100), 
	owner_id CHAR(36), 
	workspace_id CHAR(36), 
	PRIMARY KEY (id), 
	CONSTRAINT success_criteria_not_empty CHECK (jsonb_array_length(success_criteria) > 0), 
	CONSTRAINT title_length CHECK (length(title) >= 3 AND length(title) <= 255), 
	CONSTRAINT valid_mission_status CHECK (status IN ('draft', 'queued', 'in_progress', 'completed', 'blocked', 'cancelled', 'validation_failed')), 
	FOREIGN KEY(project_id) REFERENCES projects (id) ON DELETE CASCADE, 
	UNIQUE (mission_id), 
	FOREIGN KEY(result_report_id) REFERENCES reports (id) ON DELETE SET NULL, 
	FOREIGN KEY(owner_id) REFERENCES users (id) ON DELETE SET NULL, 
	FOREIGN KEY(workspace_id) REFERENCES workspaces (id) ON DELETE SET NULL
)

CREATE TABLE deepsearch_ledger_batches (
	id CHAR(36) NOT NULL, 
	mission_id CHAR(36) NOT NULL, 
	deepsearch_job_id VARCHAR(100) NOT NULL, 
	session_key VARCHAR(255) NOT NULL, 
	payload_hash VARCHAR(64) NOT NULL, 
	entry_count INTEGER NOT NULL, 
	created_at DATETIME DEFAULT (CURRENT_TIMESTAMP) NOT NULL, 
	updated_at DATETIME DEFAULT (CURRENT_TIMESTAMP) NOT NULL, 
	PRIMARY KEY (id), 
	CONSTRAINT ck_deepsearch_ledger_batches_nonempty_job CHECK (length(trim(deepsearch_job_id)) > 0), 
	CONSTRAINT ck_deepsearch_ledger_batches_nonempty_session CHECK (length(trim(session_key)) > 0), 
	CONSTRAINT ck_deepsearch_ledger_batches_hash_length CHECK (length(payload_hash) = 64), 
	CONSTRAINT ck_deepsearch_ledger_batches_entry_count CHECK (entry_count > 0 AND entry_count <= 1000), 
	CONSTRAINT uq_deepsearch_ledger_batches_mission_job UNIQUE (mission_id, deepsearch_job_id), 
	CONSTRAINT fk_deepsearch_ledger_batches_mission FOREIGN KEY(mission_id) REFERENCES missions (id) ON DELETE CASCADE
)

CREATE TABLE deepsearch_evidence_outbox (
	mission_id CHAR(36) NOT NULL, 
	deepsearch_job_id VARCHAR(100) NOT NULL, 
	deepsearch_result_key TEXT NOT NULL, 
	mission_attempt_count INTEGER NOT NULL, 
	terminal_status VARCHAR(32) NOT NULL, 
	schema_version SMALLINT DEFAULT 1 NOT NULL, 
	state VARCHAR(16) DEFAULT 'pending' NOT NULL, 
	delivery_attempt_count INTEGER DEFAULT 0 NOT NULL, 
	next_attempt_at DATETIME DEFAULT (CURRENT_TIMESTAMP) NOT NULL, 
	lease_token CHAR(36), 
	lease_expires_at DATETIME, 
	acked_at DATETIME, 
	last_http_status SMALLINT, 
	last_error_code VARCHAR(100), 
	created_at DATETIME DEFAULT (CURRENT_TIMESTAMP) NOT NULL, 
	updated_at DATETIME DEFAULT (CURRENT_TIMESTAMP) NOT NULL, 
	CONSTRAINT pk_deepsearch_evidence_outbox PRIMARY KEY (mission_id, deepsearch_job_id), 
	CONSTRAINT ck_deepsearch_evidence_outbox_nonempty_job CHECK (length(trim(deepsearch_job_id)) > 0), 
	CONSTRAINT ck_deepsearch_evidence_outbox_nonempty_result_key CHECK (length(trim(deepsearch_result_key)) > 0), 
	CONSTRAINT ck_deepsearch_evidence_outbox_positive_attempt CHECK (mission_attempt_count > 0), 
	CONSTRAINT ck_deepsearch_evidence_outbox_terminal_status CHECK (terminal_status IN ('completed', 'validation_failed')), 
	CONSTRAINT ck_deepsearch_evidence_outbox_schema_version CHECK (schema_version = 1), 
	CONSTRAINT ck_deepsearch_evidence_outbox_state CHECK (state IN ('pending', 'leased', 'acked', 'dead_letter')), 
	CONSTRAINT ck_deepsearch_evidence_outbox_delivery_attempts CHECK (delivery_attempt_count >= 0), 
	CONSTRAINT ck_deepsearch_evidence_outbox_http_status CHECK (last_http_status IS NULL OR (last_http_status >= 100 AND last_http_status <= 599)), 
	CONSTRAINT ck_deepsearch_evidence_outbox_state_coherence CHECK ((state = 'leased' AND lease_token IS NOT NULL AND lease_expires_at IS NOT NULL AND next_attempt_at = lease_expires_at AND acked_at IS NULL) OR (state = 'acked' AND lease_token IS NULL AND lease_expires_at IS NULL AND acked_at IS NOT NULL) OR (state IN ('pending', 'dead_letter') AND lease_token IS NULL AND lease_expires_at IS NULL AND acked_at IS NULL)), 
	CONSTRAINT fk_deepsearch_evidence_outbox_mission FOREIGN KEY(mission_id) REFERENCES missions (id) ON DELETE CASCADE
)

CREATE TABLE ledger_entries (
	id CHAR(36) NOT NULL, 
	project_id CHAR(36) NOT NULL, 
	mission_id CHAR(36), 
	deepsearch_batch_id CHAR(36), 
	session_key VARCHAR(255) NOT NULL, 
	origin VARCHAR(32) DEFAULT 'mcp-agent' NOT NULL, 
	claim TEXT NOT NULL, 
	summary TEXT, 
	source_url TEXT NOT NULL, 
	source_id CHAR(36) NOT NULL, 
	snippet TEXT, 
	"query" TEXT, 
	disposition VARCHAR(32) NOT NULL, 
	tags JSON DEFAULT '[]' NOT NULL, 
	owner_id CHAR(36), 
	workspace_id CHAR(36), 
	created_at DATETIME DEFAULT (CURRENT_TIMESTAMP) NOT NULL, 
	updated_at DATETIME DEFAULT (CURRENT_TIMESTAMP) NOT NULL, 
	PRIMARY KEY (id), 
	CONSTRAINT fk_ledger_entries_source_project FOREIGN KEY(source_id, project_id) REFERENCES ledger_sources (id, project_id) ON DELETE NO ACTION DEFERRABLE INITIALLY DEFERRED, 
	CONSTRAINT ck_ledger_entries_origin CHECK (origin IN ('mcp-agent', 'deepsearch-worker')), 
	CONSTRAINT ck_ledger_entries_disposition CHECK (disposition IN ('supporting', 'contradicting', 'rejected', 'background')), 
	CONSTRAINT ck_ledger_entries_nonempty_session CHECK (length(trim(session_key)) > 0), 
	CONSTRAINT ck_ledger_entries_nonempty_claim CHECK (length(trim(claim)) > 0), 
	CONSTRAINT ck_ledger_entries_nonempty_source_url CHECK (length(trim(source_url)) > 0), 
	FOREIGN KEY(project_id) REFERENCES projects (id) ON DELETE CASCADE, 
	FOREIGN KEY(mission_id) REFERENCES missions (id) ON DELETE SET NULL, 
	CONSTRAINT fk_ledger_entries_deepsearch_batch FOREIGN KEY(deepsearch_batch_id) REFERENCES deepsearch_ledger_batches (id) ON DELETE SET NULL, 
	FOREIGN KEY(owner_id) REFERENCES users (id) ON DELETE SET NULL, 
	FOREIGN KEY(workspace_id) REFERENCES workspaces (id) ON DELETE SET NULL
)

CREATE TABLE graph_edges (
	id CHAR(36) NOT NULL, 
	from_urn VARCHAR(512) NOT NULL, 
	to_urn VARCHAR(512) NOT NULL, 
	edge_type VARCHAR(50) NOT NULL, 
	direction VARCHAR(20) NOT NULL, 
	weight FLOAT NOT NULL, 
	reason TEXT, 
	via VARCHAR(50), 
	evidence JSON, 
	created_at DATETIME NOT NULL, 
	updated_at DATETIME NOT NULL, 
	PRIMARY KEY (id), 
	CONSTRAINT uq_graph_edges_from_to_type_direction UNIQUE (from_urn, to_urn, edge_type, direction)
)

CREATE TABLE users (
	id CHAR(36) NOT NULL, 
	email VARCHAR(255) NOT NULL, 
	display_name VARCHAR(255) NOT NULL, 
	password_hash VARCHAR(255) NOT NULL, 
	email_notifications_enabled BOOLEAN DEFAULT '1' NOT NULL, 
	role VARCHAR(50) NOT NULL, 
	is_active BOOLEAN NOT NULL, 
	invite_code_used VARCHAR(50),

CREATE TABLE missions (
	id CHAR(36) NOT NULL, 
	project_id CHAR(36), 
	mission_id VARCHAR(50) NOT NULL, 
	title VARCHAR(255) NOT NULL, 
	objective TEXT NOT NULL, 
	success_criteria JSON NOT NULL, 
	context JSON, 
	deliverables JSON, 
	research_phases JSON, 
	tags JSON, 
	mission_metadata JSON, 
	background TEXT, 
	focus TEXT, 
	"references" JSON, 
	required_entities JSON, 
	excluded_entities JSON, 
	expected_output_schema JSON, 
	coverage_thresholds JSON, 
	validation_thresholds JSON, 
	deliverable_format TEXT, 
	max_loops INTEGER, 
	min_loops INTEGER, 
	constraints JSON, 
	status VARCHAR(20) NOT NULL, 
	queued_at DATETIME, 
	started_at DATETIME, 
	completed_at DATETIME, 
	deepsearch_job_id VARCHAR(100), 
	deepsearch_lease_owner TEXT, 
	deepsearch_lease_token TEXT, 
	deepsearch_leased_at DATETIME, 
	deepsearch_heartbeat_at DATETIME, 
	deepsearch_lease_expires_at DATETIME, 
	deepsearch_attempt_count INTEGER DEFAULT '0' NOT NULL, 
	deepsearch_result_key TEXT, 
	execution_metadata JSON, 
	result_document_ids JSON, 
	result_report_id CHAR(36), 
	result_markdown TEXT, 
	result_protocol JSON, 
	error_message TEXT, 
	created_at DATETIME NOT NULL, 
	updated_at DATETIME NOT NULL, 
	created_by VARCHAR(100), 
	owner_id CHAR(36), 
	workspace_id CHAR(36), 
	PRIMARY KEY (id), 
	CONSTRAINT success_criteria_not_empty CHECK (jsonb_array_length(success_criteria) > 0), 
	CONSTRAINT title_length CHECK (length(title) >= 3 AND length(title) <= 255), 
	CONSTRAINT valid_mission_status CHECK (status IN ('draft', 'queued', 'in_progress', 'completed', 'blocked', 'cancelled', 'validation_failed')), 
	FOREIGN KEY(project_id) REFERENCES projects (id) ON DELETE CASCADE, 
	UNIQUE (mission_id), 
	FOREIGN KEY(result_report_id) REFERENCES reports (id) ON DELETE SET NULL, 
	FOREIGN KEY(owner_id) REFERENCES users (id) ON DELETE SET NULL, 
	FOREIGN KEY(workspace_id) REFERENCES workspaces (id) ON DELETE SET NULL
)

	mission_id CHAR(36) NOT NULL,

	payload_hash VARCHAR(64) NOT NULL,

	CONSTRAINT ck_deepsearch_ledger_batches_nonempty_session CHECK (length(trim(session_key)) > 0), 
	CONSTRAINT ck_deepsearch_ledger_batches_hash_length CHECK (length(payload_hash) = 64), 
	CONSTRAINT ck_deepsearch_ledger_batches_entry_count CHECK (entry_count > 0 AND entry_count <= 1000), 
	CONSTRAINT uq_deepsearch_ledger_batches_mission_job UNIQUE (mission_id, deepsearch_job_id),

CREATE TABLE deepsearch_evidence_outbox (
	mission_id CHAR(36) NOT NULL, 
	deepsearch_job_id VARCHAR(100) NOT NULL, 
	deepsearch_result_key TEXT NOT NULL, 
	mission_attempt_count INTEGER NOT NULL, 
	terminal_status VARCHAR(32) NOT NULL, 
	schema_version SMALLINT DEFAULT 1 NOT NULL, 
	state VARCHAR(16) DEFAULT 'pending' NOT NULL, 
	delivery_attempt_count INTEGER DEFAULT 0 NOT NULL, 
	next_attempt_at DATETIME DEFAULT (CURRENT_TIMESTAMP) NOT NULL, 
	lease_token CHAR(36), 
	lease_expires_at DATETIME, 
	acked_at DATETIME, 
	last_http_status SMALLINT, 
	last_error_code VARCHAR(100), 
	created_at DATETIME DEFAULT (CURRENT_TIMESTAMP) NOT NULL, 
	updated_at DATETIME DEFAULT (CURRENT_TIMESTAMP) NOT NULL, 
	CONSTRAINT pk_deepsearch_evidence_outbox PRIMARY KEY (mission_id, deepsearch_job_id), 
	CONSTRAINT ck_deepsearch_evidence_outbox_nonempty_job CHECK (length(trim(deepsearch_job_id)) > 0), 
	CONSTRAINT ck_deepsearch_evidence_outbox_nonempty_result_key CHECK (length(trim(deepsearch_result_key)) > 0), 
	CONSTRAINT ck_deepsearch_evidence_outbox_positive_attempt CHECK (mission_attempt_count > 0), 
	CONSTRAINT ck_deepsearch_evidence_outbox_terminal_status CHECK (terminal_status IN ('completed', 'validation_failed')), 
	CONSTRAINT ck_deepsearch_evidence_outbox_schema_version CHECK (schema_version = 1), 
	CONSTRAINT ck_deepsearch_evidence_outbox_state CHECK (state IN ('pending', 'leased', 'acked', 'dead_letter')), 
	CONSTRAINT ck_deepsearch_evidence_outbox_delivery_attempts CHECK (delivery_attempt_count >= 0), 
	CONSTRAINT ck_deepsearch_evidence_outbox_http_status CHECK (last_http_status IS NULL OR (last_http_status >= 100 AND last_http_status <= 599)), 
	CONSTRAINT ck_deepsearch_evidence_outbox_state_coherence CHECK ((state = 'leased' AND lease_token IS NOT NULL AND lease_expires_at IS NOT NULL AND next_attempt_at = lease_expires_at AND acked_at IS NULL) OR (state = 'acked' AND lease_token IS NULL AND lease_expires_at IS NULL AND acked_at IS NOT NULL) OR (state IN ('pending', 'dead_letter') AND lease_token IS NULL AND lease_expires_at IS NULL AND acked_at IS NULL)), 
	CONSTRAINT fk_deepsearch_evidence_outbox_mission FOREIGN KEY(mission_id) REFERENCES missions (id) ON DELETE CASCADE
)

	workspace_id CHAR(36),

	updated_at DATETIME DEFAULT (CURRENT_TIMESTAMP) NOT NULL, 
	PRIMARY KEY (id),

	id CHAR(36) NOT NULL, 
	project_id CHAR(36) NOT NULL, 
	mission_id CHAR(36), 
	deepsearch_batch_id CHAR(36), 
	session_key VARCHAR(255) NOT NULL, 
	origin VARCHAR(32) DEFAULT 'mcp-agent' NOT NULL, 
	claim TEXT NOT NULL, 
	summary TEXT, 
	source_url TEXT NOT NULL, 
	source_id CHAR(36) NOT NULL, 
	snippet TEXT, 
	"query" TEXT, 
	disposition VARCHAR(32) NOT NULL, 
	tags JSON DEFAULT '[]' NOT NULL, 
	owner_id CHAR(36), 
	workspace_id CHAR(36),

	updated_at DATETIME DEFAULT (CURRENT_TIMESTAMP) NOT NULL, 
	PRIMARY KEY (id),

	CONSTRAINT ck_ledger_entries_origin CHECK (origin IN ('mcp-agent', 'deepsearch-worker')),
